Risk management and control measures                                                   風險管理及控制措施

The Group adopts a prudent approach in its investments in financial assets, and        本集團在金融資產投資方面採取審慎策略，
aims to strive to achieve a balance between risk and return. The Company treats        並 致 力 於 在 風 險 與 回 報 之 間 取 得 平 衡 。本 公
protection of its capital as of utmost importance and the Company does not             司 將 資 本 保 障 視 為 首 要 要 務 ，且 不 會 進 行 任
undertake any high-risk or leveraged investments.                                      何高風險或槓桿投資。

The Company considers that effective risk management is crucial to protecting          本 公 司 認 為 ，有 效 的 風 險 管 理 對 於 保 護 本 集
the Group’s capital and achieving its investment goals. The Company has                團 的 資 本 及 實 現 其 投 資 目 標 至 關 重 要 。本 公
adopted a systematic and real-time approach to track investment performance            司已採用系統化且實時的方法來追蹤投資表
and market conditions, providing immediate insights into ever-changing and             現 及 市 場 狀 況 ，從 而 能 即 時 掌 握 瞬 息 萬 變 及
emerging risks. The executive Directors are responsible for the risk management        新 興 的 風 險 。執 行 董 事 負 責 本 集 團 投 資 的 風
of the Group’s investment, and with assistance from the investment team, will          險 管 理 ，並 在 投 資 團 隊 的 協 助 下 ，定 期 檢 討
regularly review the risk parameters of each investment. The investment team           各 項 投 資 的 風 險 參 數 。投 資 團 隊 由 具 備 財 務
comprises experienced professionals with expertise in financial analysis, portfolio    分 析 、投 資 組 合 管 理 及 風 險 評 估 專 業 知 識 的
management and risk assessment, and conduct day-to-day risk management                 資 深 專 業 人 士 組 成 ，並 負 責 執 行 日 常 的 風 險
work.                                                                                  管理工作。

measures to mitigate risks. The market prices, latest news and significant             主 動 措 施 以 減 輕 風 險 。團 隊 每 日 監 控 市 場 價
market events are monitored on a daily basis, while credit risk, liquidity risk and    格 、最 新 新 聞 及 重 大 市 場 事 件 ，同 時 亦 不 時
concentration risk are also monitored from time to time.                               監控信貸風險、流動性風險及集中風險。

and relevant legal and regulatory requirements. Any breaches will be reported to       與 監 管 要 求 的 遵 守 情 況 。任 何 違 規 行 為 將 呈
the Board for corrective action.                                                       報予董事會，以便採取糾正措施。

The investment decision making process and approval and                               投資決策流程及審批與監督機制
oversight mechanisms

The typical implementation cycle for investments is outlined below:                   投資的典型執行週期概述如下：

Step 1: Identify investment opportunities                                             步驟1： 物色投資機會

          Potential investment opportunities are identified and sourced by the            投資團隊透過市場研究、行業會議，
          investment team through various channels, including market research,            以及來自業界聯絡人及╱或金融中介
          industry conferences, and referrals from industry contacts and/or               機構推薦等多種渠道，物色並尋求潛
          financial intermediaries.                                                       在投資機會。

Step 2: Process initial project screening                                             步驟2： 進行初步項鍊篩選

          The executive Directors and the investment team perform an initial              執行董事及投資團隊將根據本集團採
          screening of such potential investment based on predefined criteria             納的投資政策所訂定的預先標準，對
          pursuant to the investment policies adopted by the Group.                       該潛在投資進行初步篩選。

Step 3: Conduct due diligence                                                         步驟3： 進行盡職審查

          For those potential investments that pass initial screening, the                對於通過初步篩選的潛在投資，本公
          investment team of the Company will conduct detailed due diligence,             司投資團隊將進行詳細的盡職審查，
          including financial modelling, valuation analysis, assessment of                包括財務模型建構、估值分析、管理
          management quality, industry and competitive landscape analysis, and            層質素評估、行業與競爭格局分析，
          legal and regulatory reviews.                                                   以 及 法 律 與 監 管 審 查（ 如 認 為 屬 必
                                                                                          須）。

Step 4: Assess risk and implication                                                   步驟4： 評估風險與影響

          Each individual investment will be discussed among the executive                每 項 投 資 均 會 由 執 行 董 事 、投 資 團
          Directors, investment team, finance department and legal department.            隊、財務部門及法務部門共同商討。
          Decisions are reached through collaborative discussions, where                  決策乃透過協作討論達成，過程中各
          members share their insights and expertise. For instance, the                   成員會分享其見解與專業知識。舉例
          investment team presents their analysis and recommendation about                來說，投資團隊會就投資要點與風險
          the highlights and risks, the finance department assesses the financial         提出分析與建議，財務部門則評估財
          and accounting implications, and the legal department assesses legal            務與會計層面的影響，而法務部門則
          and compliance risks. The executive Directors will participate in the           評估法律與合規風險。執行董事將參
          discussions, where they will review the investment materials, and               與討論，期間將審閱投資資料，並提
          follow-up questions or require additional information (if necessary). On        出 後 續 問 題 或 要 求 提 供 補 充 資 料（如
          certain occasions, the Company will seek advice from external advisors          有 需 要 ）。在 若 干 情 況 下 ，倘 本 公 司
          (such as financial adviser) in respect of the investment if it considers        認為必要，將就該項投資徵詢外部顧
          necessary.                                                                      問（例如財務顧問）的意見。

Step 5: Prepare investment proposal                                                   步驟5： 擬定投資提案

          Through thorough discussions, consensus is sought regarding the                 透過深入討論，針對投資的潛在風險
          potential risks and rewards of an investment, and decisions are made            與回報尋求共識，並根據集體意見作
          based on collective input. Once a decision is reached, the investment           出決策。一旦達成決策，投資團隊將
          team will present the proposal to the Board. The proposal includes              向董事會提交提案。提案包含投資分
          investment analysis, expected risks and returns, alignment with                 析、預期風險與回報、與策略的一致
          strategy, due diligence findings, proposed investment amount, and exit          性、盡職審查結果、建議投資金額及
          strategy, together with the relevant documentation.                             退出策略，並附上相關文件。

yet opportunistic outlook. While we anticipate that the absence of new mega-         審 慎 且 具 前 瞻 性 展 望 。儘 管 我 們 預 計 缺 乏 新
infrastructure project commencements will continue to weigh on the machinery         啟 動 巨 型 基 建 工 程 項 目 ，短 期 內 將 繼 續 對 機
rental segment in the short term, the Group is strategically positioned for          械 租 賃 分 部 造 成 一 定 壓 力 ，但 本 集 團 已 穩 踞
a recovery anchored by the Northern Metropolis. As site formations and               策 略 位 置 ，以「北 部 都 會 區 」作 為 支 撐 ，迎 接
engineering works for this landmark development commence towards the end of          市 場 復 甦 。隨 著 這 項 標 誌 性 發 展 項 目 的 工 地
2026, we expect a significant turnaround in fleet utilization and demand.            平整及建設工程將於二零二六年年底展開，
                                                                                     我們預期機隊利用率與需求將會大幅回升。

To navigate this transitional period, we are implementing a dual-track fleet         為了順利渡過這段過渡期，我們正實施「雙軌
strategy. By increasing our inventory of cost-effective machinery, we can            機 隊 策 略 」。透 過 增 加 具 成 本 效 益 機 械 的 庫
offer more competitive rental rates to capture price-sensitive market share.         存 ，我 們 能 夠 提 供 更 具 競 爭 力 的 租 賃 價 格 ，
Simultaneously, we will retain a specialized portfolio of high-quality Japanese      從 而 爭 取 對 價 格 敏 感 的 市 場 份 額 。與 此 同
and European equipment to satisfy premium clients who prioritize reliability and     時 ，我 們 將 會 保 留 由 高 質 量 日 本 及 歐 洲 設 備
safety for complex infrastructure tasks.                                             組 成 的 專 業 設 備 組 合 ，以 滿 足 該 等 在 複 雜 基

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                                       企業管治常規

The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and maintaining high              本公司及董事會致力達致及維持最高水平之
standard of corporate governance as the Board believes that effective corporate      企 業 管 治 ，董 事 會 相 信 ，行 之 有 效 的 企 業 管
governance practices are fundamental in enhancing the shareholder value              治常規對提升股東價值以及保障股東及其他
and safeguarding the interests of the Shareholders and other stakeholders.           權 益 相 關 者 之 利 益 至 關 重 要 。因 此 ，本 公 司
Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound corporate governance principles           已 採 納 健 全 之 企 業 管 治 原 則 ，當 中 著 重 優 秀
that emphasise a quality Board, effective internal control, stringent disclosure     之 董 事 會 、有 效 之 內 部 監 控 、嚴 謹 之 披 露 常
practices and transparency and accountability to all Shareholders.                   規以及對所有股東之透明度及問責性。

The Company has complied with the code provisions set out in the Corporate           本 公 司 已 遵 守 上 市 規 則 附 錄 C1所 載 之 企 業 管
Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules,       治 守 則（「企 業 管 治 守 則 」）之 守 則 條 文 ，惟 偏
except for the deviation from the code provision C.2.1.                              離守則條文第C.2.1條除外。

Under code provision C.2.1, the roles of chairman and chief executive should         根 據 守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 ，主 席 與 行 政 總 裁 之
be separate and should not be performed by the same individual in order to           角 色 應 有 區 分 ，並 不 應 由 同 一 名 人 士 兼 任 ，
ensure that there is clear division of responsibilities between the chairman         以確保董事會主席與本公司行政總裁之職責
of the Board and the chief executive of the Company. The Company has not             有 明 確 區 分 。本 公 司 並 無 委 任 主 席 ，而 董 事
appointed a chairman and the Board provides leadership for the Company.              會 則 領 導 本 公 司 。考 慮 到 本 集 團 於 重 大 時 刻
Having considered the business operation of the Group at the material time, it is    之 業 務 營 運 ，本 公 司 認 為 董 事 會 由 經 驗 豐 富
believed that the Board, which consists of experienced professionals, can function   之 專 業 人 士 組 成 ，整 體 可 有 效 運 作 ，而 執 行
effectively as a whole, while the executive Directors along with other members       董事連同本公司其他高級管理人員則有能力
of senior management of the Company are capable of overseeing the day-to-day         監督本集團在現有穩固企業管治架構下之日
management of the Group under the strong corporate governance structure in           常管理工作。
place.

Nevertheless, the Company understands the importance of compliance with the          儘 管 如 此 ，本 公 司 明 白 遵 守 企 業 管 治 守 則 之
code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code and will continue to review the structure        守 則 條 文 第 C.2.1條 十 分 重 要 ，並 將 繼 續 不 時
from time to time and consider appointing a chairman/chairlady of the Board as       檢 討 有 關 架 構 ，亦 考 慮 在 適 當 時 委 任 董 事 會
and when appropriate.                                                                主席。

The Group commits to continuously improving its corporate governance practices       本集團承諾透過定期檢討持續改善其企業管
by periodic review to ensure that the Group continues to meet the requirements of    治常規以確保本集團繼續達到企業管治守則
the CG Code.                                                                         之要求。

the Group and is collectively responsible for promoting the success of the          導 及 監 督 本 集 團 事 務 ，集 體 負 責 促 進 本 集 團

effectiveness of the internal control system and supervising and managing the       監督及管理管理層之表現。
management’s performance.

audit matters. We describe these matters in our auditor’s report unless law or       法 律 或 法 規 不 容 許 公 開 披 露 此 等 事 項 ，或 於
regulation precludes public disclosure about the matter or when, in extremely        極 罕 見 的 情 況 下 ，本 核 數 師 認 為 由 於 可 合 理
rare circumstances, we determine that a matter should not be communicated in         預期披露此等事項的不良後果將超越公眾
our report because the adverse consequences of doing so would reasonably be          知 悉 此 等 事 項 的 利 益 ，因 而 不 應 於 報 告 中 披
expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such communication.             露 ，否 則 本 核 數 師 會 於 核 數 師 報 告 中 說 明 此

3.     BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED                                                3.   編製綜合財務報表之基準及重大
       FINANCIAL STATEMENTS AND MATERIAL                                                        會計政策資料
       ACCOUNTING POLICY INFORMATION

       3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                                       3.1 編製綜合財務報表之基準
           statements

              The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance            綜合財務報表已按香港會計師公會頒佈
              with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. For the purpose of preparation of               之香港財務報告準則編製。就編製綜合
              the consolidated financial statements, information is considered material         財務報表而言，如果合理預期此類資料
              if such information is reasonably expected to influence decisions made            會影響主要使用者所作的決策，則該資
              by primary users. In addition, the consolidated financial statements              料 被 視 為 重 大 。此 外 ，綜 合 財 務 報 表 包
              include applicable disclosures required by the Listing Rules and by the           括上市規則及香港公司條例之適用披露
              Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                                                    規定。

              The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the                   誠如下文載列之重大會計政策資料所闡
              historical cost basis, except for investment properties and certain               釋，除投資物業及若干金融工具於各呈
              financial instruments that are measured at fair values at the end                 報期末按公平值計量外，綜合財務報表
              of each reporting period, as explained in the material accounting                 乃按歷史成本基準編製。
              policy information set out below.

              Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the                       歷史成本一般以交換貨品及服務時所付
              consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                           代價之公平值為基準。

              Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or                公平值為於計量日期市場參與者在有序
              paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market             交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債所支
              participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that price            付之價格，而不論該價格為直接可觀察
              is directly observable or estimated using another valuation technique.            或使用其他估值技術估計得出。

              A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account              非金融資產之公平值計量須計及市場參
              a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by                   與者能自最大限度使用該資產達致最佳
              using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another           用途，或將該資產出售予將最大限度使
              market participant that would use the asset in its highest and best               用該資產達致最佳用途之其他市場參與
              use.                                                                              者所產生之經濟效益。

       3.2 Material accounting policy information                                               3.2 重大會計政策資料

              Basis of consolidation                                                                綜合基準

              The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                       綜合財務報表包括本公司、由本公
              statements of the Company and entities controlled by the Company                      司控制之實體及其附屬公司之財務
              and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the Company:                           報表。本公司於下列情況下即擁有
                                                                                                    控制權：

              •      has power over the investee;                                                   •    有權控制被投資對象；

              •      is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                        •    就其參與被投資對象之可變
                     involvement with the investee; and                                                  回報承受風險或享有權利；
                                                                                                         及

              The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                           本 集 團 於（或 當 ）完 成 履 約 責 任 時
              obligation is satisfied, i.e., when “control” of the goods or services            （即 於 與 特 定 履 約 責 任 相 關 的 貨 品
              underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred to                或 服 務 的「控 制 權 」轉 移 予 客 戶 時 ）
              the customer.                                                                     確認收益。

              A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle                履約責任指一項明確貨品或服務
              of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or           （或一批貨品或服務）或一系列大致
              services that are substantially the same.                                         相同的明確貨品或服務。

              Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over                     倘符合以下其中一項條件，則控制
              time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of                  權為隨時間轉移，而收益則參考完
              the relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria is             全履行相關履約責任的進展情況隨
              met:                                                                                時間確認：

              •      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the                        •    客戶於本集團履約時同時收
                     benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group                         取及消耗本集團履約所提供
                     performs;                                                                         的利益；

              •      the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that                    •    本集團的履約創造或提升客
                     the customer controls as the Group performs; or                                   戶於本集團履約時控制的資
                                                                                                       產；或

              •      the Group’s performance does not create an asset with                        •    本集團的履約並無創造對本
                     an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                              集團具有替代用途的資產，
                     enforceable right to payment for performance completed to                         而本集團擁有強制執行權收
                     date.                                                                             取至今已履約部分的款項。

              Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                        否則，收益於客戶獲得明確商品或
              customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.                           服務控制權時確認。

              Taxation                                                                          稅項

              Income tax expense represents the sum of current and deferred                     所得稅開支指即期及遞延所得稅開
              income tax expense.                                                               支的總和。

              The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.                即期應付稅項是根據本年度之應課
              Taxable profit differs from loss before tax because of income or                  稅溢利計算。應課稅溢利與除稅前
              expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and items that              虧損存在差異是由於應課稅溢利不
              are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax            包括其他年度之應課稅收入或可扣
              is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively             稅支出項目，且不包括永遠毋須課
              enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                       稅及不可扣稅項目。本集團的即期
                                                                                                稅項負債按報告期末已頒佈或實質
                                                                                                頒佈的稅率計算。

              Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between the                   遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表中資
              carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated                    產及負債之賬面值及用於計算應課
              financial statements and the corresponding tax bases used in the                  稅溢利的相應稅基之間的暫時差額
              computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally             確認。一般情況下，所有因應課稅
              recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets             暫時差額而產生之遞延稅項負債均
              are generally recognised for all deductible temporary differences                 予確認。遞延稅項資產一般按所有
              to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will be available          可扣稅暫時差額確認，惟須以將很
              against which those deductible temporary differences can be                       可能有應課稅溢利可使用該等可扣
              utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised             稅者時差額予以抵扣為限。如暫時
              if the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other            差 額 是 因 某 交 易 初 次 確 認（業 務 合
              than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in a                    併 除 外 ）其 他 資 產 及 負 債 而 產 生 ，
              transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting            又不影響應課稅溢利及會計溢利，
              profit and at the time of the transaction does not give rise to equal             且交易時並不同時產生等額的應課
              taxable and deductible temporary differences. In addition, deferred               稅暫時差額及可扣減之暫時差額，
              tax liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises             則不予以確認此等遞延稅項資產及
              from the initial recognition of goodwill.                                         負債。此外，倘暫時差額在初始確
                                                                                                認商譽時產生，則遞延稅項負債將
                                                                                                不予確認。

              Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary                     除非本集團可控制有關暫時差額之
              differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and                       回撥及暫時差額在可見將來都不可
              associates, except where the Group is able to control the reversal                能回撥，否則遞延稅項負債均須就
              of the temporary difference and it is probable that the temporary                 於附屬公司及聯營公司之投資有關
              difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax               之應課稅暫時差額予以確認。與該
              assets arising from deductible temporary differences associated                   等投資及權益相關的可扣稅暫時差
              with such investments and interests are only recognised to the                    額產生之遞延稅項資產僅於可能有
              extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient taxable profits          足夠應課稅溢利用以動用暫時差額
              against which to utilise the benefits of the temporary differences and            利益及預期於可預見將來可撥回時
              they are expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.                           予以確認。

              (ii)    Definition of default                                                        (ii)     違約的定義

                      For internal credit risk management, the Group considers                              就內部信貸風險管理而言，
                      an event of default occurs when information developed                                 本集團認為當內部產生或獲
                      internally or obtained from external sources indicates that the                       取自外部來源的資料表明，
                      debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group, in                      債 務 人 不 太 可 能 向 債 權 人（包
                      full (without taking into account any collaterals held by the                         括 本 集 團 ）全 額 還 款（不 考 慮
                      Group).                                                                               本 集 團 持 有 的 任 何 抵 押 品 ），
                                                                                                            即發生違約事件。

                      Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default                           除 上 述 情 況 外 ，本 集 團 認
                      has occurred when a financial asset is more than 90 days                              為 ， 倘 金 融 資 產 逾 期 超 過 90
                      past due unless the Group has reasonable and supportable                              天，則發生違約事件，除非本
                      information to demonstrate that a more lagging default                                集團具有說明更寬鬆的違約
                      criterion is more appropriate.                                                        標準更為合適的合理可靠資
                                                                                                            料，則作別論。

              (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                             (iii)    信貸減值金融資產

                      A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events                         金融資產在一項或以上事件
                      that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash                          （對 該 金 融 資 產 估 計 未 來 現 金
                      flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a                         流 量 構 成 不 利 影 響 ）發 生 時 維
                      financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable data                          持 信 貸 減 值 。金 融 資 產 維 持
                      about the following events:                                                          信貸減值的證據包括有關下
                                                                                                           列事件的可觀察數據：

                      (a)    significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                          (a)   發行人或借款人的重大
                             borrower;                                                                            財政困難；

                      (b)    a breach of contract, such as a default or past due                            (b)   違反合約（如違約或逾期
                             event;                                                                               事件）；

                      (c)    the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                                 (c)   借款人的貸款人因有關
                             contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                                       借款人財政困難的經濟
                             financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a                               或合約理由而向借款人
                             concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                                 批出貸款人不會另行考
                             consider; or                                                                         慮的優惠；或

                      (d)    it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                           (d)   借款人將可能陷入破產
                             bankruptcy or other financial reorganization.                                        或其他財務重組。

                     Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.                     回的任何款項於損益中確認。

                     The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of                      預期信貸虧損之計量為違約

                     there is a default) and the exposure at default. The assessment                 損 大 小 ）及 違 約 時 風 險 敞 口 之
                     of the probability of default and loss given default is                         函 數 。違 約 概 率 及 違 約 虧 損
                     based on historical data and forward-looking information.                       之評估乃基於過往數據按前
                     Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                         瞻 性 資 料 作 調 整 。預 期 信 貸

                     practical expedient in estimating ECL on trade receivables                      違約的風險為權重而釐定。
                     using a provision matrix taking into consideration historical                   本集團經考慮過往信貸虧損
                     credit loss experience and forward looking information that is                  經驗及毋須花費不必要成本
                     available without undue cost or effort.                                         或精力即可獲得的前瞻性資

                                                                                                     損。

                     Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual                    一 般 而 言 ，預 期 信 貸 虧 損 為

                     discounted at the effective interest rate determined at initial                 收取的現金流量之間的差

                      The fair value of the Project Land was determined using direct                                 項目土地的公平值採用直接比較法
                      comparison method, based on recent market transaction data of sales                            釐定，基於類似性質及條件所銷售
                      of land with similar nature and condition and adjusted for differences in                      土地的最近市場交易數據，並根據
                      the location of the land. The fair value measurement is categorised into                       土地位置的差異作出調整。公平值
                      Level 3 fair value hierarchy.                                                                  計量分類至公平值架構等級第3級。

                      In estimating the fair value of the land, the highest and best use of the                      估計土地之公平值時，土地之最高
                      land is its current use.                                                                       及最佳用途為其現時用途。

                      One of the key unobservable inputs used in valuing the fair value of the                       於截至二零二五年三月三十一日止

                      Cambodian Riel (“KHR”) 96,000, equivalent to HK$187 per square meter                           用的重大不可觀察輸入數據之一為
                      for the year ended 31 March 2025.                                                              每平方米的經調整價格，其平均值
                                                                                                                     為 每 平 方 米 96,000柬 埔 寨 瑞 爾（「 柬
                                                                                                                     埔寨瑞爾」）  （相當於187港元）。

                      Based on the result of the valuation, the recoverable amount of the                           根據估值結果，截至二零二五年三
                      Project Land was KHR1,652,000 million, equivalent to HK$3,213 million                         月三十一日止年度，項目土地可收
                      for the year ended 31 March 2025, accordingly an impairment loss of                           回 金 額 為 1,652,000百 萬 柬 埔 寨 瑞 爾
                      HK$425 million (of which the Group’s share of loss was HK$94 million)                         （相 當 於 3,213百 萬 港 元 ），因 此 ，截

              On 2 September 2024, the Group entered into an agreement (the “Agreement”)                 二零二四年九月二日，本集團與獨立第三
              with independent third parties, to invest in Neway (Hong Kong) Limited (“Neway”)           方訂立協議（「協議」）以對龍威（香港）有限

              Based on the agreement signed with the fund manager, the Group was liable to                根據與基金管理人簽訂的協議，本集團應
              pay a total of approximately HK$27 million to the fund manager as part of the               向基金管理人支付總計約27百萬港元，作

              During the year ended 31 March 2024, the Group had a legal dispute with the                 於截至二零二四年三月三十一日止年度
              fund manager, where the fund manager asked for settlement of approximately                  期 間 ，本 集 團 與 基 金 管 理 人 發 生 法 律 糾
              HK$27 million while the Group counter argued the fund manager had been                      紛，基金管理人要求結清約27百萬港元，
              mishandling the unlisted debt instruments, accordingly the Group was seeking                而本集團反駁稱基金管理人對非上市債
              for refund of the original investment costs of approximately HK$7 million and               務工具處理不當，因此本集團要求退還原

       Credit risk and impairment assessment                                                  信貸風險及減值評估

       Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties default on their        信貸風險指本集團對手方就其合約責任
       contractual obligations resulting in financial losses to the Group. The                違約導致本集團遭受財務損失的風險。
       Group’s credit risk exposures are primarily attributable to trade receivables,         本集團的信貸風險主要歸因於貿易應
       other receivables and deposits, loan receivables, finance lease receivables,           收 款 項 、其 他 應 收 款 項 及 訂 金 、應 收 貸
       corporate note receivables and bank balances. The Group does not                       款 、融 資 租 賃 應 收 款 項 、應 收 企 業 票 據
       hold any collateral or other credit enhancements to cover its credit risks             及銀行結餘。本集團並無持有任何抵押
       associated with its financial assets except that the credit risks associated           品或其他信貸增強工具以覆蓋與其金融
       with certain loan receivables are mitigated because they are secured over              資產有關的信貸風險，惟與若干應收貸
       properties.                                                                            款有關的信貸風險因其有物業抵押而有
                                                                                              所減輕。

       and 70.7% (2025: 51.7%) of the total trade receivables was due from                及70.7%（二零二五年：51.7%）的貿易應收

       within securities brokerage segment. In order to minimise the credit               五大客戶，而該等客戶屬於證券經紀分

35. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF FINANCIAL                                                 35. 金融工具公平值計量（續）
    INSTRUMENTS (CONTINUED)

       Some of the Group’s financial instruments are measured at fair values for            就財務報告用途，本集團部分金融工具
       financial reporting purposes.                                                        按公平值計量。

       In estimating the fair values, the Group uses market-observable data to the          於估計公平值時，本集團會使用可取得
       extent it is available. For instruments with significant unobservable inputs         的市場可觀察數據。針對含有第3級重大
       under Level 3, the Group engages third party qualified valuers to perform            不可觀察輸入數據之工具，本集團委聘
       the valuation.                                                                       第三方合資格估值師進行估值。

       Some of the Group’s financial assets and financial liabilities are measured          本集團部分金融資產及金融負債於各報
       at fair values at the end of each reporting period.                                  告期末按公平值計量。

       The directors of the Company consider that the carrying amounts of                   本公司董事認為，於綜合財務報表按攤
       financial assets and financial liabilities at amortised cost recognised in the       銷成本確認之金融資產及金融負債之賬
       consolidated financial statements approximate to their fair values.                  面值與彼等之公平值相若。
